    "title": "Fusion Financial Cloud Processes for Learners",
    "description": "Finance being the central repository of the ERP System, the branch of study includes multiple processes. To ensure the learners understand the processes in a phased manner, some of the basic flows are offered in this expertise. ",

  {
    "id": "cmk51hurw0007hf2ge0jich46",
    "title": "Fusion Financial Cloud Processes for Practitioners",
    "description": "Once the learners are confident on the basic flows in the Fusion Financial Cloud, they can take a look at the critical process where additional configurations can be done based on the business requirement.
